Secure Living  -  Final Landlord Dashboard Specification (Unified for Developer)
This document defines the landlord dashboard for Secure Living. It integrates rent management, escrow handling, compliance, risk monitoring, communication, and due diligence services in one flow. The design ensures simplicity for local landlords while scaling to advanced needs for diaspora clients and premium modules.

1. Top Bar Overview (At a Glance)
A compact overview always visible at the top of the dashboard. Provides landlords with immediate confidence on financial health, occupancy, and compliance.
 Current Month Rent --> Collected vs. Expected (KES + %)
 Occupancy Rate (%) --> Active vs. vacant units
 Cash Reserve Balance --> Amount with % progress toward landlord's target
 Escrow Health Status --> ✅ Healthy / ⚠️ Warning / 🚨 Critical
 Next Compliance Due --> e.g., "Land Rates: 14 days left"

2. Priority Notifications (Alert Inbox)
High-visibility alerts, color-coded by severity, always at the top.
Priority Notifications & Notification Framework
The notification system is the backbone of communication in Secure Living. It ensures landlords, tenants, and external clients receive the right alerts, at the right time, without unnecessary duplication.
Priority Inbox (Landlord Dashboard View)
The Priority Inbox appears at the top of the landlord dashboard, color-coded by severity:
 🚨 Critical (Immediate Action Required)
 "Unit A4: Rent 14 Days Overdue"
 "Cash Reserve Below Minimum (3% vs. 5% required)"
 "Land Rates overdue (KES 18,000)"
 ⚠️ Warnings (Grace Period)
 "Maintenance Request pending >7 days (Unit B2)"
 "Business Permit renewal due in 7 days"
 ✅ Resolved (Archive)
 "Funds released for refund dispute (Tenant X)"
 "NEMA certificate uploaded by proxy"
Action Buttons: Acknowledge | View Details | Escalate | Pay Now | Assign Proxy

Notification Framework (System Logic)
Supported Channels
 In-App Push Notifications  -  Default for all app users.
 Email Alerts  -  Used for diaspora landlords and formal documents.
 SMS  -  Short, urgent alerts (overdue rent, escrow breach).
 WhatsApp (Opt-in)  -  Quick confirmations (rent received, visit reminders).
Notification Categories
 Financial Alerts
 Rent received / overdue.
 Escrow balance & reserves.
 Landlord payouts processed.
 Operational Alerts
 Maintenance requests (created, delayed, resolved).
 Vacancy updates: "Upcoming" --> "Available Now."
 Service provider job status.
 Compliance & Proxy Alerts
 Land rates & permits due.
 Tax filing reminders.
 Court / NEMA / county case updates.
 Risk & Safety Alerts
 GIS risk warnings (flood, encroachment).
 Security incidents flagged by staff.
Priority & Escalation Rules
 🚨 Critical --> Always SMS + push + email.
 ⚠️ Warning --> Push + email.
 ℹ️ Informational --> Push only.
 If critical alert unacknowledged in 24h --> escalate via all available channels.
User Controls
 Channel preferences (SMS, WhatsApp, email).
 Quiet hours (no non-critical alerts at night).
 Frequency (real-time or daily digest).
Admin & Audit Features
 Delivery tracking (sent / delivered / read).
 Escalation logs to prove legal notice delivery.
 Full audit trail linked to property_id & user_id.

3. Financial & Escrow Overview
Detailed visibility into rent and escrow flows.
 Rent Collection Graph --> Collected vs. Outstanding
 Escrow Allocations --> Rent, Management Fee, Reserves, Utilities
 Upcoming Payouts --> Dates, beneficiaries, amounts
 Refund Requests --> Tenant refunds in progress
Escrow Allocation Logic
 Tenant pays rent --> Escrow_Master
 Automatic splits:
 Management Fee (base %)
 Add-On Services (Tax, Security, etc.)
 Shared Utilities (tenant/landlord share)
 Reserve % (if activated)
 Remainder --> Landlord Wallet
 Fines --> Allocated by company policy
Active Services Panel in the landlord dashboard --> showing subscribed add-ons, next renewal dates, fees deducted

Hybrid Escrow Mode
In some cases, tenants may pay rent directly to landlords (e.g., via personal M-Pesa). 
To avoid data gaps, the platform should allow landlords or supervisors to log such payments 
into the system. These will not flow through escrow but will still appear in dashboards, 
ensuring consistency and gradual migration toward full escrow compliance.
Outcome: transparent, rules-driven fund flows.

4. Property Performance
Analytics showing property health and tenant behavior.
 Occupancy & Vacancy Trendline --> Last 12 months
 Tenant Turnover --> Move-ins vs. Move-outs
 Payment Timeliness --> Avg. days early/late
 Unit Breakdown --> Vacant | Overdue | Active (quick filters)
Landlords see patterns, not just numbers.

5. Risk & KPI Dashboard
Deeper analytics and oversight.
 Core KPIs
 Rent Arrears % (trendline)
 Tenant Retention Rate (% > 1 year)
 Escrow Accuracy (target 100%)
 Staff/Professional Performance Score
Exception Alerts (system-detected anomalies)
 🚨 Escrow safeguard triggered  -  attempted release failed system rules
 ⚠️ Duplicate maintenance request detected
 ✅ All checks passed this month
 These include duplicate invoices, unusual expense claims, or escrow mismatches. Neutral language preserves trust while flagging risks.
 Risk Heatmap
 Disputes per 100 tenants
 Compliance alerts (e.g., tax filing pending)
 Process irregularities flagged for admin
 Suggested Actions
 "Consider early lease renewal for Unit B4"
 "Review Professional James  -  3 late uploads this month"
 "Escrow mismatch flagged  -  system resolved automatically"
Purpose: protect funds, highlight risks, and suggest proactive steps.

6. Communication Center (Noticeboard)
Central hub for all structured communication.
 Manager --> Tenant Notices --> e.g., water outage, rent increase
 Tenant Feedback Inbox --> Complaints, confirmations, service requests
 Landlord <--> Manager Messaging --> Secure in-app chat
Keeps all communication logged and auditable.

7. Documents & Legal
Digital archive of agreements, receipts, and compliance papers.
 Digital Agreements --> Lease terms, escrow policies, refund rules
 Signed Contracts Archive --> With timestamps
 Downloadable Reports --> Monthly landlord statements, tax-ready summaries
Every important document is stored, timestamped, and retrievable.

8. Compliance & Proxy Dashboard
Organized workspace for regulatory obligations and proxy services.
A. Compliance Overview
 Next Due Item (e.g., Land Rates, Tax Filing)
 Overdue Compliance Count
 Active Proxy Requests
B. Compliance Alerts
Integrated into Priority Inbox (Section 2).
 🚨 Land Rates overdue  -  Unit B3, KES 18,000
 ⚠️ Tax return deadline in 14 days
 ✅ NEMA certificate uploaded
Compliance Prioritization:
To prevent confusion, compliance items should be grouped into two categories:
:: Mandatory for Property Type (e.g., fire inspection for commercial properties, land rates for all properties).
:: Optional but Recommended (e.g., project oversight, GIS boundary mapping).
This ensures landlords clearly understand what they must comply with versus what is an optional service.
C. Active Compliance Items Table
Columns: Property | Type | Due Date | Status | Assigned To | Cost | Action
Filters: Overdue | Due Soon | Completed | GIS Verified
D. Due Diligence Checklist (One-Time Services)
Landlord selects required services, each priced separately:
 Title Deed / Ownership Verification
 Boundary Confirmation (GIS)
 Valuation & Market Benchmarking
 NEMA Filing
 County Permits
 Proxy Representation (Court / County)
Workflow: Selection --> Quote --> Escrow Payment --> Task Completion --> Archive
E. Proxy Assignment
 Assign Secure Living or vetted professional
 Task updates: In Progress --> Completed
 Deliverables uploaded into Documents & Legal
F. Compliance Badges & Expiry
 Badges: GIS Verified, Tax Filed, NEMA Compliant
 Expiry reminders: 30 / 14 / 7 / 1 days
 Badges can also display on property listings (optional)
G. Compliance Archive
 Timeline of all compliance actions per property
 Downloadable Compliance Pack (Receipts + Certificates + Reports)
Outcome: landlords stay compliant without confusion; diaspora owners get peace of mind.
 Professional Coordination: Landlords can view assigned professionals, track milestones, and approve work through the integrated Professionals Action Panel (Section 4.5).


Critical Features for smooth flow
These features, providing deeper technical and operational clarity. Each item now includes workflow, system logic, and integration notes for the developer.

1. Cross-Posting Vacancies to External Channels
Workflow:
 Tenant gives vacating notice --> Unit flagged "Upcoming Vacancy."
 Supervisor confirms --> Unit status "Available."
 Landlord opts-in to "Cross-Post Listing."
 System pushes details (photos, rent, amenities, GPS) to selected channels.
System Logic:
 Auto-sync status (Available --> Rented).
 API/webhooks integration with external platforms (e.g., Facebook Marketplace, Jiji).
 "Cross-posted" flag visible in landlord dashboard.
Impact:
 Expands reach beyond Secure Living.
 Faster filling of units --> reduced vacancy loss.

2. Move-In / Move-Out Digital Checklists
Workflow:
 At tenant move-in: Supervisor completes condition checklist + photos.
 Stored in system under unit's lease profile.
 At move-out: Supervisor re-checks same items.
 System auto-generates comparison (Before vs. After).
 Deposit refund decision linked to checklist.
System Logic:
 Checklist stored in structured JSON format.
 Geotagged photos & timestamps required.
 Dashboard shows "Deposit Release Conditions Met / Not Met."
Impact:
 Transparent, evidence-based refunds.
 Reduces disputes with tenants.

3. Inspection & Maintenance Report Generator
Workflow:
 Supervisor performs scheduled or ad-hoc inspection.
 Uploads findings (condition score, notes, photos).
 System generates standardized PDF report.
 Report auto-stored in landlord dashboard & tenant file.
System Logic:
 Template-driven PDF generation.
 Version control (Inspection #1, #2, etc.).
 Reports tagged by purpose (insurance, routine, emergency).
Impact:
 Landlords receive professional-grade documentation.
 Adds credibility in disputes, claims, or resale discussions.

4. Tenant Notifications & Saved Searches
Workflow:
 Tenant saves search profile (location, rent, unit type, amenities).
 When new unit matches: system auto-sends alert.
 Tenant clicks notification --> pre-filled inquiry form --> landlord notified.
System Logic:
 Search profiles stored per tenant.
 Matching triggered by property status change.
 Notification channels: in-app, SMS, email (based on tenant preference).
Impact:
 Keeps tenants engaged even when their first choice isn't available.
 Reduces vacancy downtime with ready demand pool.

 Vendor Marketplace (Lightweight Version)
Provider Verification Tiers:
To protect landlords and tenants from poor service, all vendors should carry verification badges:
:: Basic = uploaded ID only.
:: Silver = active trade license on file.
:: Gold = escrow-compliant and performance-reviewed.
For the MVP, Secure Living will only onboard pre-approved vendors until the marketplace matures.
Workflow:
 Landlord clicks "Find Professional" from dashboard.
 Searches by category (plumber, electrician, cleaning).
 Sees provider profiles (contact, rating, verification status).
 Requests a quote or books directly.
System Logic:
 Provider directory table with verification tier.
 Simple booking form linked to landlord + property ID.
 Notifications to provider via SMS/email.
Impact:
 Gives landlords quick access to trusted vendors.
 Builds foundation for later escrow-secured marketplace.


✅ Developer Notes
 Compliance data is a dedicated section but alerts also surface in Priority Notifications.
 All compliance-related payments must route through escrow.
 Deliverables (receipts, certificates) auto-store in Documents & Legal.
 Landlords can release payment if they are satisfied  --  all workflows are system or proxy managed.

🔒 Premium Features Gate:
python
def can_access_premium_insight(user, insight_type):
    if user.tier == "advanced" or user.tier == "diaspora_premium":
        return True
    elif user.has_active_premium_subscription:
        return True
    else:
        return False

Landlord Insights Hub (Secure Action Panel)
Transforming Secure Living into a Strategic Partner
The Landlord Insights Hub is a modular, data-driven intelligence ecosystem that shifts landlords from reactive property management to proactive wealth optimization. It provides personalized, actionable insights through tiered access, engagement-based progression, and premium advisory features.
The Hub is built around trust (transparent ROI), habit (gamified engagement scoring), and loyalty (landlords can see exactly how Secure Living grows their returns).

Core Architecture
Pipeline Overview
Portfolio Data --> Machine Learning Models --> Tier & Engagement Filtering --> Subscription Gate --> Dashboard Delivery
     ↓                     ↓                           ↓                        ↓                     ↓
Property events     Predictive analytics         Tier logic (Essentials,     Premium paywall     Cards, Alerts,
& transactions      (rent, churn, market)        Professional, Portfolio)    (Strategic Suite)   Reports & Charts
Inputs:
 Property database (leases, payments, utilities, vacancies)
 Tenant behavior (timeliness, support tickets, churn signals)
 Market data APIs (rent benchmarks, permits, trends)
 Professional performance (staff, vendors, supervisors)
Outputs:
 Real-time dashboards
 Predictive alerts & opportunities
 Monthly ROI reports
 Personalized engagement feedback

Multi-Tier Insights Framework
Classification is based on portfolio size and engagement score. This makes the system fair and drives usage.
Tier 1  -  Essentials (Core Insights)
(Default for 1 - 2 properties | Free)

 Vacancy alerts & quick-fill suggestions
 Lease renewal countdowns

(3 - 10 properties OR high engagement | Free)
 Rental yield comparisons across units
 Tenant churn prediction & retention scoring
 Repair cost trending vs neighborhood averages
 Renewal incentive recommendations
 Portfolio performance benchmarking
 Professional/vendor ratings
Tier 3  -  Portfolio Intelligence
(10+ properties OR high engagement growth | Free)

 CapEx planning & ROI forecasts

 Acquisition/disposition signals

Tier 4  -  Strategic Advisory (Premium: "Wealth Optimization Suite")
(KES 3,500/month or KES 33,600/year)
 Market forecasting & gentrification alerts

2. Lease & Payments
 Full transparency + downloads.
 Lease Summary: unit code, start / end date, rent, deposit, lease document (signed).

 Refund Requests / Move-out Refund Tracker: status (Draft --> Submitted --> Under Review --> Approved --> Paid Out) with timestamps and documents.
 Auto-linking: If vacating notice submitted, lease_end_date is flagged and visible here.
 
3. Vacating Notice 
 UI element and business flow for tenants to give notice.
 Tenant UI (Tenant App / Dashboard)
 Submit Vacating Notice button (visible only when lease active).
 Form fields:
 Planned move-out date (datepicker)  --  system validates required notice period (configurable, e.g., 30 days).
 Reason for leaving (dropdown: Too Expensive / Relocation / Condition / Job Transfer / End of Contract / Other + optional comment).
 Preferred refund disbursement method (Bank / M-Pesa / Wallet)  --  must be pre-verified.
 Optional: forwarding address (text).
 Consent checkbox: "I understand this triggers inspection & draft listing creation." (timestamped)
 System Actions on Submission
 vacating_notice_submitted event logged.
 Lease flagged ending_on = planned_move_out_date.
 Unit status --> Upcoming Vacancy.
 Auto-draft listing created and linked to auto_listing_id.
 Supervisor receives auto-task to schedule inspection (configurable SLA; recommended default: 72 hours post notice or within X days of vacate).
 Landlord notified (with ability to pre-approve premium listing options).
 Notify-Me subscribers (search profiles matching the unit) are queued for notification when status changes to "Available Now".
 Tenant Options after Submission
 Cancel Notice (allowed up to a configurable cutoff; action logs vacating_notice_cancelled).
 Edit Planned Move-out Date (only if within allowed timeframe and with audit log).
 
 4. Utilities & Services
 No change in principle, but link to vacating.
 Current contributions list; contributions remain until lease end unless landlord updates.
 On vacating notice, the system shows pro-rated utilities for final invoice (based on meter readings).
 Garbage/estate fixed fees treated separately (displayed and computed per landlord config).
 
5. Notices & Communication Hub (Refined)
Centralized hub for all tenant-facing alerts, notices, and communications.

 Rent payment history & downloadable receipts.

 Email (for diaspora tenants).

6. Support & Requests (Move-out specific)
 Maintenance + dispute resolution tied to move-out.
 Report a Maintenance Issue (photo upload) --> work order.
 Move-out Inspection Scheduling: tenant receives inspection slot proposal; accepts or requests reschedule.
 Dispute Protocol: if supervisor/landlord proposes deductions, tenant receives deduction list (with photos) and can accept or raise dispute (pauses refund release).
 
7. Documents
 Lease, receipts, POA (if diaspora), inspection report.
 Lease Contract: digitally signed copy (download).
 Inspection Reports: once completed, the final inspection report is attached to tenant profile (photos + supervisor notes + condition checklist).
 Refund Documents & Deductions: itemized and downloadable.
 
8. Tenant Preferences & Settings
 Controls tenant communications and privacy.
 Notification Method: SMS / Email / In-app (chosen).
 Reminder Days: e.g., 3 / 7 days before due date.
 Refund Disbursement: M-Pesa / Bank / Wallet (must be verified and stored securely).
 Privacy Consent: opt-in/out for anonymized benchmarking and market insights.
 
9. Move-out & Refund Calculation (Business Rules)
 Clear deterministic formula and audit trail.
 Meter reading process: caretaker reports reading --> supervisor inputs initial_reading (on move-in) and final_reading (on move-out) or supervisor captures both at inspection. Upload meter photos for verification.
 Billing formula: Units Used = final_reading − initial_reading
Consumption Cost = Units Used x Price per Unit
Total Bill = Consumption Cost + Fixed Meter Charge + Any Separate Fees (e.g., garbage)
 Final Statement & Deductions: security deposit less approved deductions = refund. Deductions require evidence (photos/invoices) and supervisor sign-off.
 Refund Release: release from escrow only after inspection_status == Completed and any dispute windows closed or resolved.
 
10. Transparency & Auditability
 Everything is auditable and timestamped.
 Events logged: vacating_notice_submitted, vacating_notice_cancelled, inspection_scheduled, inspection_completed, draft_listing_created, refund_requested, refund_processed.
 Audit trail: who uploaded photos, who approved deductions, timestamps for each decision.
 Tenant access: tenant can view the full timeline and supporting evidence before any refund is released.
 
11. UX & Messaging Recommendations (what tenants see & when)
 Small UX touches reduce confusion.
 After submission: show a confirmation page summarizing next steps (inspection, timeline, draft listing).
 Dashboard banner: "You have submitted a vacating notice  --  click to view inspection schedule or cancel notice."
 Proactive alerts: supervisor message when inspection scheduled, landlord notification about premium listing options.
 When listing is filled: auto-message to saved/subscribed tenants: "Unit has been taken. Would you like similar notifications?"
 
12. Developer Notes  --  Fields & DB mapping 
 Add or extend these fields / relationships.
 tenant_profiles (existing)
 Add: vacating_notice_submitted (bool), notice_submission_date (timestamp), planned_move_out_date (date), vacate_reason (enum), refund_disbursement_method (enum), refund_status (enum), auto_listing_id (nullable FK), inspection_status (enum: none/scheduled/completed/pending), notice_cancelled (bool), notice_cancelled_at (timestamp).
 tenant_leases (existing)
 Add or update: lease_end_date (becomes editable when notice submitted but tracked as historical change), final_invoice_id (FK), move_out_inspection_id (FK).
 inspections table (new / existing)
 inspection_id, property_id, unit_id, scheduled_time, completed_time, supervisor_id, photos (URLs), condition_score, notes, meter_initial, meter_final, verification_status.
 listings / auto_listings
 auto_listing_id, source_event (vacating_notice_id), status (draft / available_from / available_now / filled / archived), created_at, created_by.
 events / event_store
 Log events listed in section 10; each event includes event_id, event_type, user_id, role, property_id, payload, timestamp.
 escrow_deposits
 refund_request_id, refund_amount_calculated, refund_status, evidence_urls, approved_by.
 
13. Required Validations & Business Rules
 Prevent mistakes and protect all parties.
 Notice date validation: planned move-out must respect configured notice period. If not, reject or prompt with warning.
 Cancel rule: tenant can cancel up to cutoff (configurable). Cancellation logs vacating_notice_cancelled.
 Supervisor-only actions: caretakers cannot upload or confirm inspection  --  they only forward evidence. Supervisors must verify meter readings and sign inspection.
 Escrow gating: refunds only released after inspection completed and dispute window resolved; otherwise status = On Hold.
 Mandatory fields: refund disbursement method must be verified before refund processed.
 Notifications: landlord and saved-search subscribers notified at key status transitions.
 
14. Events to Add (for developer mapping)
 Add to your event appendix for analytics and automation:
 vacating_notice_submitted
 vacating_notice_cancelled
 auto_listing_created
 inspection_scheduled
 inspection_completed
 final_invoice_generated
 refund_requested
 refund_approved
 refund_paid
 unit_marked_filled
 notify_me_triggered (for subscriber alerts)
 Each event should include actor_id, role, property_id, unit_id, related_ids, and a timestamp.
 
15. Linking to Listings & Media
 How tenant profile changes feed the rest of the system.
 When tenant submits notice: Section 15 auto-draft listing created (auto_listing_id) and uses existing photos per G.8 rules.
 Supervisor inspection completeness triggers listing status Available Now and media update if new photos uploaded.
 When lease is signed, unit_marked_filled archives listing and triggers save/subscriber notifications.
 
 16. KPIs & Monitoring (tenant-side)
 Track success of move-out flows and tenant satisfaction.
 Average notice-to-inspection time (target within SLA).
 Average notice-to-listing live time.
 Refund turnaround time (from inspection complete to refund paid).
 Dispute rate for refunds (lower is better).
 Tenant NPS at move-out (trend per property/portfolio).
 
17. Example Tenant Flow (concise)
 Tenant clicks Submit Vacating Notice --> form validated --> vacating_notice_submitted logged.
 System flags lease ending, auto-creates draft listing and notifies supervisor & landlord.
 Supervisor schedules inspection --> inspection_scheduled event. Tenant accepts slot.
 Supervisor completes inspection, uploads photos, meter readings --> inspection_completed. System calculates final invoice.
 If all good, refund_approved and funds released per refund_disbursement_method. Unit status --> Available Now.
 Tenant signs new lease elsewhere; system marks unit Filled when new lease signed, archives listing and notifies subscribers.

✅ Why This Matters:

 Insights Hub doesn't replace these behaviors  --  it complements them by reducing uncertainty, improving communication, and making tenants feel respected.
 This builds tenant loyalty, which indirectly boosts landlord retention and Secure Living's brand reputation.





Secure Living Escrow System Blueprint

1. Wallets vs. Escrow_Subwallets Relationship
 wallets table = source of truth for current balances (real-time spendable money).
 escrow_subwallets table = immutable ledger (audit log of how funds were allocated per transaction).
Flow:
 On allocation, update wallets.balance.
 Insert a record into escrow_subwallets describing the split.
 Nightly reconciliation job:
 SELECT wallet_id, SUM(amount) 
 FROM escrow_subwallets 
 WHERE date >= today 
 GROUP BY wallet_id
Compare result with wallets.balance. If mismatch --> alert Ops.
✅ This ensures balances remain tamper-proof but efficient to query.

2. Handling the "Remainder" in Allocation
Define calculation explicitly:
Net Rent to Landlord = rent_amount 
                     - management_fee 
                     - landlord_covered_utilities 
                     - reserve_percent_allocation
 management_fee = Base Commission % of rent.
 landlord_covered_utilities = Garbage/Security Lighting/Water for common areas (if landlord pays).
 reserve_percent_allocation = Contribution to Reserve_Escrow (if configured).
👉 The remainder = clean net payout to Landlord_Wallet.

3. Error Handling & Retry Logic (State Machine)
 Transactions must follow strict state transitions:
 PENDING --> ALLOCATING --> ALLOCATED --> RELEASED  
 PENDING --> ALLOCATING --> FAILED --> (RETRY 3 times or MANUAL REVIEW)  
 PENDING --> CANCELLED (if invalid)
 Idempotency Key: txn_id ensures no double allocation.
 Retry Logic:
 If allocation step fails (e.g., can't move to Utilities_Escrow), rollback to PENDING.
 System retries up to X times.
 If still failing --> mark as FAILED and create Ops alert.
 If allocation fails, retry 3 times, then escalate to Ops Lead for manual intervention.
 Ops Action Panel: Failed transactions must display root cause + manual "Retry" button.

4. Indexing for Performance (DB Scalability)
Critical indexes:
 transactions(lease_id, status) --> Fast lookup of pending/failed payments per tenant.
 tenant_utilities(lease_id) --> Quickly fetch utilities tied to a lease.
 invoices(lease_id, due_date) --> Efficient billing cycle queries.
 escrow_subwallets(txn_id) --> Speed reconciliation and audits.
👉 Without these, monthly invoicing at scale will become a bottleneck.

5. Enhanced Ops Action Panel  -  Reporting Suite
The admin dashboard should include prebuilt financial reports:
 Landlord Statement
 Period summary of rent collected, fees, costs, and payouts.
 Export to PDF/Excel.
 Tenant Ledger
 Full history of charges, payments, and disputes.
 Required for tenant transparency and legal compliance.
 Escrow Reconciliation Report
 Daily check: Escrow_Master == SUM(escrow_subwallets).
 Highlight mismatches in red.
 Should be downloadable for auditors/regulators.


3. KPI Adjustments
 Standard Management
 Landlord Payout Timeliness: >=95% (depends on tenant payments).
 Fine Recovery Rate: Reported but fines don't affect landlord income.
 Advance Payout Guarantee
 Landlord Payout Timeliness: 100% (non-negotiable).
 Fine Recovery Rate: Internal KPI only (since fines go to Secure Living).

4. Developer Notes
 UI Logic:
 If APG selected --> Hide "Fine Type" and "Grace Period" fields.
 If Standard Management --> Show full fine configuration menu.
 Ledger Logic:
 APG landlords always receive payout on due date.
 Late fines auto-allocated to Company Wallet, not landlord wallet.
 Contract Integration:
 Digital agreement must reflect landlord's enrollment choice clearly (no disputes later).
Title: Monthly Funds Allocation and Disbursement

Actors: AccountingSystem, Database, PaymentGateway, Administrator

| AccountingSystem | Database      | PaymentGateway | Administrator |
|------------------|--------------|----------------|---------------|
|                  |              |                |               |
| allocateFunds()  |              |                |               |
|----------------->|              |                |               |
|                  |              |                |               |
|        [Processes Allocation]   |                |               |
|                  |              |                |               |
|                  | updateBalances() |             |                |
|                 -|------------->|                |               |
|                  |              |                |               |
|                  |  commitSuccess() |             |                |
|                 <-|-------------|                |               |
|                  |              |                |               |
| alt              |              |                |               |
|   [Success]      |              |                |               |

|   ----------------------------->|                |               |
|                  |              |                |               |
|   [Failure]      |              |                |               |

C. Professional Workflow Diagram
flowchart TD
    Request[Client Request Service] --> Assign{Assign Professional?}
    Assign -->|Internal| InternalProfessional[Secure Living Staff Professional]
    Assign -->|External| ExternalProfessional[Verified External Professional]
    InternalProfessional --> Supervise[Optional Staff Supervision]
    ExternalProfessional --> Supervise
    Supervise --> Report[Professional Submits Report + Docs]
    Report --> Wallet[Wallet Triggers Payment/Release]
Key Notes:
 Clients don't see multiple dashboards; instead, they choose services and the system assigns Professionals.
 Internal Professionals are always priority for sensitive tasks (e.g., property purchases).
 External Professionals handle overflow or specialized tasks, with optional supervision by Secure Living staff.

D. Airbnb Mode Workflow
flowchart TD
    Guest[Airbnb Guest Books Property] --> Payment[Airbnb or Direct Payment]
    Payment --> Trust[Secure Living Trust Account]
    Trust --> LandlordWallet[Landlord Wallet Ledger]
    Professional[Professional Manages Airbnb] --> Report[Guest Stay Report]
    LandlordWallet -->|After Checkout| Landlord[Funds Released]

Airbnb as "Special Tenant":
 When Airbnb Mode is toggled for a property, guests are treated like temporary tenants.
 Professionals manage check-ins, cleaning, damage reports.
 Funds are held until checkout confirmed & damage-free approval.



Detailed Developer Instructions (Mapped to Diagrams)
✅ Frontend Guidelines:
 React/Next.js with role-based dashboards:
 Landlord Dashboard: Portfolio, rent reports, repair approvals.
 Tenant Dashboard: Rent payments, repair requests.
 Professional Dashboard: Task queue, report upload.
 Admin Dashboard: Supervision, dispute resolution, analytics.
✅ Backend Guidelines:
 Use Laravel Jetstream for user management & authentication.
 Build API endpoints for every key flow shown in diagrams.
✅ Wallet & Ledger Rules:
 Every payment is tagged: (Who paid --> What for --> Who receives).
 Trust Account is mirrored in ledger (every landlord sees "their money").
 Payment is released after "The client (landlord/tenant) approves the completed work" via the dashboard. The client's approval is the final milestone that triggers the wallet release to the marketplace provider.
✅ Professional Assignment Logic:
 Internal Professionals default for sensitive transactions.
 External Professionals used for:
 Overflow
 Specialist services (e.g., rural inspections)
 Cost-conscious clients (cheaper tier)
✅ Airbnb Mode:
 Add toggle in property profile.
 When ON --> system:
 Changes "Tenant" workflow to "Guest" workflow.
 Assigns Professionals to manage check-ins/cleaning.
✅ Service Provider Mini-Action Panel:
 MVP: Providers respond via WhatsApp/SMS links.
  Add basic dashboard (task acceptance, upload receipts/photos).


💻 Developer Implementation Guide
System: Real-Time Feedback --> Risk Register --> Contingency Playbook --> SOP/Training

1. System Architecture Overview
Think of this as a closed loop system:
 Feedback Collection --> from Professionals, staff, tenants, landlords.
 Data Storage & Processing --> structured into a feedback_table linked to tasks, users, and transactions.
 Analysis Engine --> detects patterns, escalates risks, and updates the Risk Register.
 Action Layer --> activates playbooks, updates SOPs, and pushes alerts.
 Learning Layer --> uses feedback data in training & KPI dashboards.

Links to users (responsible person)

3. Workflow Triggers
Professional Workflow
 ON_TASK_COMPLETE --> Prompt: "Was this task straightforward? (Y/N) Any issues?"
 Store in feedback_table.
 If "N" or rating <=2 --> flag for Ops review.
Tenant Workflow
 ON_REPAIR_CLOSED --> Prompt: "Rate repair (1 - 5). Was technician professional?"
 If rating <=2 --> Auto-generate support ticket.
 Tenant release payment to Artisan
Landlord Workflow
 ON_REPORT_DELIVERED / FUNDS_RELEASED --> Prompt: "Rate clarity/timeliness (1 - 5)."
 If rating <=3 --> Tag staff ID + escalate to KPI dashboard.
 The Landlord releases payment to the Artisan
Staff Workflow
 REPORT_HURDLE button in dashboard.
 Logs inefficiency or barrier --> stored in feedback_table --> flagged for Ops.

4. Analysis Engine
 Keyword Clustering: NLP tags repeated issues (e.g., "water meter," "report delay").
 Threshold Alerts:
 10+ mentions of the same keyword/week --> create/update risk in risk_register.
 Escrow complaints >5 in a week --> trigger Escrow Playbook.
 Sentiment Analysis: Auto-tag feedback tone. Negative spikes escalate risk.

5. Dashboards (Frontend Components)
a. Operational Insights Dashboard (Ops Lead)
 Top 5 recurring issues (keyword clusters).
 Risk Register live status (Green/Amber/Red).
 "New SOP Recommendations" auto-suggested from feedback.
b. KPI Dashboard (HR/Training)
 Staff/Professional scores over time.
 Triggered auto-enrolments in training modules.
 Tenant Reliability Index (TRI) updates.
c. Leadership Dashboard
 Risk Register summary (likelihood x impact heatmap).
 Decision Log (sortable by outcome).
 Contingency Fund health (financial buffer view).

6. SOP & Training Integration
 Auto-SOP Update Suggestions:
 Feedback clustering --> generates recommended SOP change draft.
 Example: 15 Professionals flagged "water meter missing" --> system suggests: "Add water meter photo requirement in inspection SOP."
 Training Modules:
 If Professional "professionalism" <70% --> auto-assign "Professional Conduct" training.
 If staff "report clarity" <70% --> assign "Advanced Reporting" training.

7. Contingency Playbook Integration
 Each risk_register item links to a Playbook JSON file.
Example: Escrow Failure Playbook JSON
{
  "trigger": "Escrow complaints > 5 per week OR API check fails",
  "actions": [
    "Freeze fund releases",
    "Alert CEO & CFO",
    "Run manual reconciliation",
    "Send pre-recorded client reassurance video"
  ],
  "owner": "CFO",
  "escalation_time": "Immediate"
}

8. Developer Notes (Critical)
 APIs: Ensure all tasks (repairs, reports, payments) can fire ON_COMPLETE hooks.
 Storage: Use cloud DB with strong audit trails. No feedback should be editable once logged.
 UI/UX: Feedback prompts must be frictionless (1-click + optional text).
 Security: Sensitive logs (like fraud suspicions) visible only to Ops/Leadership.
 Backups: Risk Register & Decision Log must have automated daily backups.

Foreign Settler Services  -  System Integration Blueprint (Phase 1)
(Add under Premium Concierge Services)
1. Purpose & Strategic Positioning
The Secure Living Foreign Settler Services (FSS) module is designed as a digital-first concierge and facilitation service for foreigners relocating to Kenya and international investors seeking to purchase, manage, or develop property remotely.
It is built to operate fully online, requiring no physical office or onsite presence.
FSS integrates seamlessly with the core Secure Living ecosystem and leverages existing workflows, vendor networks, escrow systems, and community resources to offer a trusted, structured, and transparent onboarding experience for foreign clients.
This module strengthens Secure Living's authority globally and positions the platform as a one-stop solution for relocation, investment, and property management in Kenya.

2. System Overview & Integration Structure
Foreign Settler Services should be implemented as a new service category within Secure Living's modular, taxonomy-based structure. It must function using the same underlying logic as other premium services such as:
 Construction Oversight
 Escrow Workflows
 Vendor Marketplace
 Proxy Services
 Property Management
Core System Placement
Premium Concierge Services --> Foreign Settler Services (FSS)
No separate app is needed -- this module uses existing system infrastructure.

3. Key Use Cases
A. Foreigners Relocating to Kenya
The platform provides:
 Immigration/work permit support
 Housing search coordination
 School search & community integration
 Banking setup guidance
 Security & service provider onboarding
 Settling-in support (utilities, transport, documentation)
B. Foreign Investors (Remote Buyers)
The platform offers:
 Property sourcing
 Title verification and legal oversight
 Lawyer & valuer coordination
 Escrow-based safe purchase
 Construction oversight (if building)
 End-to-end property management and tenant onboarding

4. System Features for the Developer (Technical Implementation)

This role should have:
 Own onboarding flow

 CRM tagging (foreign_lead, investor_lead, relocation_lead)

 Multi-step checklists

Premium Concierge Service  --  Foreign Settler Services
Implementation (MVP)  --  Developer Specification
Purpose: provide a fully detailed, developer-ready specification for launching the Secure Living Foreign Settler Services MVP: a high-touch, web-first concierge that onboards foreigners (expats, diaspora, investors) into Kenya and provides document, legal, property search, and settlement support. The module must be modular, secure, auditable, and prepared for integration with the main property-management system in future phases.

1. Scope (MVP)
In scope (deliver now):
 Public-facing landing page + service request form
 Backend storage of requests (case file)
 Admin dashboard (case list, detail view, assignment, status changes)
 File upload & secure storage (documents)
 Internal notes / communication log per case
 Email notifications (user confirmation, admin alert, status change)
 Basic role: Admin / Settler Officer (optional)
 Minimal CRM fields: contact, nationality, purpose, services requested, budget, timeline
 Status workflow (New --> In Review --> In Progress --> Completed --> Closed)
 Simple assignment & audit logs
 Simple reports (count by status / country / service category)
 Security: input validation, file validation, RBAC
 Future hooks (webhooks, API endpoints) for later automation
Out of scope (defer or stub):
 Payment gateway integration (add as future hook)
 Automated KYC/CRB checks (stub endpoints)
 Full integration with property listings or wallet
 WhatsApp Business integration (optional future)
 AI automation (future)

2. Primary Actors & Roles
 Guest / Prospective Settler  --  fills public form; can upload docs.
 Admin / Operations  --  full access to Settler dashboard; assign cases, change status.
 Settler Services Officer (optional)  --  limited access to manage assigned cases.
 System  --  notification sender, storage, audit logger.
RBAC must be simple now (Admin vs SettlerOfficer vs Guest), extensible later.

3. UX / UI Requirements (Screens & Components)
Public
 Landing Page (Concierge > Foreign Settler Services)
 Title, short intro, packages summary, FAQs, testimonials
 CTA button --> Service Request Form
 Service Request Form
 Single-page or multi-step (mobile-first)
 Fields (required vs optional):
 Full name (required)
 Email (required, validate)
 Phone (required, international format)
 Nationality (required)
 Purpose (dropdown: Relocation / Investment / Business Setup / Long-term Rental Search / General Inquiry)
 Services (multi-select): Visa & Legal, KRA PIN & Docs, Bank Account, House Search, Property Purchase, Due Diligence, Construction Oversight, Short-Term Settling Assistance
 Budget Min / Max (optional numeric/currency)
 Preferred move-in / timeline (optional date)
 Preferred contact channel (Email / Phone / WhatsApp)
 Additional notes (long text)
 Document uploads (optional; passport scan, preliminary docs)
 GDPR-style consent checkbox (consent text stored)
 Validation: required fields, file size limits, allowed file types (pdf, jpg, png), CAPTCHA optional
 Post-submit: show confirmation page + auto-email
Admin / Internal
3. Settler Dashboard (Admin Panel)
 Table list with columns: Request ID, Name, Nationality, Services, Budget, Timeline, Status, Assigned To, Created At
 Filters: Status, Country, Service Category, Assigned Staff, Date Range
 Search by name, email, phone, request id
 Bulk actions: assign, export (CSV), change status
 Request Detail View
 Top: summary card (contact, nationality, purpose, brief)
 Middle: services requested, timeline, budget
 Right: action pane (Assign, Change Status, Create Task, Schedule Call)
 Tabs:
 Notes (internal)  --  timestamped, author, attachments
 Documents  --  list with download, preview; secure link expiry
 Activity log  --  audit trail (who changed what, when)
 Communication (future): email thread/WhatsApp log
 Buttons: Add Internal Note, Upload Document, Send Email (templated), Assign Officer, Change Status
 Simple Reports Page
 KPI tiles: total requests, by status, by country, by service type
 Export CSV

4. Data Model (Minimal DB Tables)
All tables use UUID primary keys. Timestamps: created_at, updated_at.
A. settler_requests
 id (UUID)
 request_number (string, human-readable)
 name (string)
 email (string)
 phone (string)
 nationality (string)
 purpose (enum)
 services (json)  --  array of selected service keys
 budget_min (int) nullable
 budget_max (int) nullable
 timeline (date) nullable
 preferred_contact (enum: email/phone/whatsapp)
 notes (longtext)  --  initial free-text from user
 status (enum: new, in_review, awaiting_client, in_progress, completed, closed)
 assigned_to (user_id nullable)
 consent_given (boolean)
 source (string)  --  marketing or manual tag
 created_at, updated_at
B. settler_documents
 id (UUID)
 request_id (UUID) FK
 file_name, file_path, file_size, file_type
 uploaded_by (enum: guest/admin)
 uploaded_by_user_id nullable
 description (string)
 created_at
C. settler_notes
 id (UUID)
 request_id (UUID)
 author_user_id (UUID)
 note_text (longtext)
 private (boolean default true)
 created_at
D. settler_activity_log
 id (UUID)
 request_id (UUID)
 actor_user_id nullable
 action_type (string) e.g., status_change, assign, upload, email_sent
 metadata (json)
 created_at
E. users (existing table)
 ensure fields for roles, permissions; allow assigned_to mapping
F. settings / templates
 email templates (settler_confirmation, status_change), stored in DB

5. API Endpoints (Public & Admin)
Authentication: JWT for admin endpoints; public form endpoint is unauthenticated but rate-limited.
Public / Client
 POST /api/settler/requests  --  create request (accepts multipart for files) --> returns request_id, status 201
 GET /api/settler/requests/{id}/status  --  (optional) public status check (requires token + privacy controls)
Admin / Internal
 GET /api/admin/settler/requests  --  list + filters
 GET /api/admin/settler/requests/{id}  --  detail
 POST /api/admin/settler/requests/{id}/assign  --  assign staff (body: user_id)
 PATCH /api/admin/settler/requests/{id}  --  update status, budget, timeline
 POST /api/admin/settler/requests/{id}/notes  --  add internal note
 POST /api/admin/settler/requests/{id}/documents  --  upload document (admin)
 GET /api/admin/settler/requests/export  --  CSV export
 POST /api/admin/settler/requests/{id}/email  --  send templated email (body: template_key, extra_message)
Design endpoints for idempotency and proper validation. Return structured errors: {code, message, details}.

6. Notifications & Email Templates
Events to notify:
 On submission: send user confirmation email + admin alert email
 On assignment: email to assigned officer (optional)
 On status change: email to user (if opted in)
 On document upload (if user uploaded): email confirmation
 Weekly digest for admin: new requests summary (optional)
Example templates to implement:
 settler_request_received  --  includes request id, summary, next steps
 settler_status_change  --  includes new status, assigned officer details
 settler_assignment  --  assigned to you: link to admin dashboard
Store templates in DB with placeholders ({{name}}, {{request_id}}, {{status}}, {{link}}).

7. Security & Compliance
 Input sanitation on all user inputs. Use server-side validation in addition to client-side.
 File uploads: limit file types to pdf, jpg, jpeg, png, max 10MB per file. Scan for malware if possible. Use randomized file names and store in S3/CDN with short-lived signed URLs for downloads.
 Access control: only Admin or assigned SettlerOfficer can view request details or private docs. Public endpoints must not return PII.
 Consent & Logging: store consent boolean and exact consent text version. Keep immutable audit trail of changes (settler_activity_log).
 Encryption: at-rest encryption for documents and sensitive fields (PII) per policy. TLS for transit.
 Retention: configurable retention policy for uploaded docs (e.g., 7 years by default  --  configurable).
 GDPR-style: implement data subject request hooks (export personal data, delete request with admin approval) for future compliance.

8. Performance & Non-Functional Requirements
 Form submission latency < 3s under nominal load.
 Admin dashboard page load < 1.5s for first page (use server-side pagination).
 File uploads via presigned S3 or chunking for reliability.
 Use caching (Redis) for dashboard summary counts (invalidate on create/change).
 Queue background jobs for heavy tasks (email sends, file virus scan, PDF generation). Use Laravel queue/RabbitMQ/Sidekiq style.

9. Acceptance Criteria (QA)
Feature: Submit service request
 Given valid input, when user submits, then request created in DB, confirmation email sent to user, admin notified.
Feature: Admin list & filter
 Admin can filter by status, country, service type; results match DB.
Feature: Add internal note
 Note saved with author id and timestamp; visible only to admin/assigned officer.
Feature: File upload
 Allowed types accepted, stored securely, accessible only by authorized users via signed URL.
Feature: Status change audit
 Changing status creates an activity_log entry with actor_user_id and timestamp.
Security: Unauthorized cannot access admin endpoints.
Provide unit tests and e2e tests for public form and admin flows.

10. Developer Implementation Notes & Timeline Estimate
Tech Stack suggestions (consistent with Masterpiece): Laravel backend, Postgres DB, Vue/React admin SPA, S3 for storage, Redis for cache/queues, Mailgun/SendGrid for email.
Suggested iterations (2 - 4 sprints):
 Sprint 1 (1 week): Landing page + public form + DB table + email confirmation + admin list (basic).
 Sprint 2 (1 week): Request detail view, internal notes, document upload, assign functionality, status workflow.
 Sprint 3 (1 week): Templates, exports, reporting tiles, audit logs, acceptance tests.
 Sprint 4 (1 week): Hardening: security review, validations, retention policies, small UX polish.
Total: 3 - 4 weeks for MVP depending on availability.

11. Future Hooks (must not block)
Design the module so later features can be plugged in without schema changes:
 Payment / invoice module (connect to wallet)
 Calendly or scheduling widget integration
 WhatsApp Business message flow (webhook)
 KYC providers (Onfido-like) and e-signature for agreements
 API exposure for partner referral systems
 Link to property listing module and tenant onboarding flows
Include external_reference and integration_status fields in settler_requests to map future external IDs.

12. Testing & QA Checklist
 Form field validation tests (server + client).
 File upload acceptance and download tests.
 RBAC tests for admin vs officer visibility.
 API rate-limiting tests (public form).
 Email deliverability tests (templates).
 Load test for typical peak: 200 concurrent users.
 Security scan and S3 signed URL test.

13. Documentation for Handoff
Provide short docs for:
 DB schema (ERD PNG)
 API spec (OpenAPI/Swagger) for all endpoints above
 Admin usage guide (screenshots)
 Deployment steps (env variables for S3, email, queue, DB)
 Maintenance: cron jobs, queue workers, backups

14. Acceptance / Handover Checklist for Launch
 All acceptance criteria passed (see section 9)
 OpenAPI spec published and accessible to dev/test teams
 Basic admin accounts seeded for ops team
 Email deliverability verified (no spam blocks)
 Backup & retention configured
 Security review passed (basic OWASP checks)
 Production deployment tested on staging

Secure Living  -  Developer Guide: Integrated Feedback --> Risk --> SOP --> KPI System

1. Database Schema (Core Tables)
Create modular tables that link tasks, users, and risks.
a. Feedback Table
CREATE TABLE feedback (
    feedback_id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id INT NOT NULL,              -- Professional, staff, tenant, landlord
    role ENUM('Professional','staff','tenant','landlord'),
    task_id INT,                       -- links to specific job/milestone
    transaction_id INT,                -- for escrow/money related
    rating INT,                        -- 1-5 stars, optional
    sentiment ENUM('positive','neutral','negative'),
    open_text TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW(),
    flagged BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE      -- true if needs Ops review
);
b. Risk Register Table
CREATE TABLE risk_register (
    risk_id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    title VARCHAR(255),
    category ENUM('operational','market','internal'),
    likelihood INT,            -- 1 - 5
    impact INT,                -- 1 - 5
    trigger_condition TEXT,    -- e.g., "Competitor captures >5% market share"
    status ENUM('open','monitored','mitigated','closed'),
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW()
);
c. Contingency Playbook Table
CREATE TABLE contingency_playbook (
    playbook_id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    risk_id INT REFERENCES risk_register(risk_id),
    trigger TEXT,
    immediate_action TEXT,
    communication_template TEXT,
    resolution_steps TEXT,
    owner_id INT,                  -- staff responsible
    last_drill DATE,
    next_drill DATE
);
d. SOP & Training Log Table
CREATE TABLE sop_training_updates (
    update_id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    source ENUM('feedback','risk_review','post_mortem'),
    description TEXT,
    action_owner INT,
    training_required BOOLEAN,
    linked_kpi VARCHAR(255),
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW()
);

2. Feedback Collection (UX Layer)
Embed prompts into existing workflows:
 Professional Dashboard:
 After task completion --> Popup:
 "Was this straightforward? Y/N"
 "Any unexpected issues?" (text)
 Tenant Action Panel:
 After repair closure --> 1 - 5 stars + comments.
 Landlord Action Panel:
 After report sent or funds released --> 1 - 5 stars + optional feedback.
 Staff Dashboard:
 "Report a Hurdle" button --> logs inefficiency in real time.
⚡ Developer Note: Use lightweight modal popups or inline forms. Store responses directly into feedback table with role + task IDs.

3. Feedback --> Risk Register Automation
Set up keyword clustering + triggers:
 If >3 negative feedbacks in 30 days about same Professional --> auto-create a risk: "Professional Performance Degradation."
 If multiple landlords select "Cost Too High" in cancellation dropdown --> risk: "Pricing Strategy Challenge."
 Use a cron job (daily/weekly) to parse feedback.open_text with NLP library (e.g., Python's spaCy) to detect common terms.
Result: Risk auto-added into risk_register.

4. Contingency Playbook Integration
 Each risk in risk_register links to at least one playbook.
 Example:
 Risk: Escrow API Failure
 Trigger: if escrow_api_status = down for >15 minutes
 Immediate Action: Freeze payouts, alert CFO.
 Communication: Pre-drafted email + pre-recorded founder video.
⚡ Developer Note: Implement system alerts via Slack + email with playbook_id attached.

5. SOP & Training Updates
Every risk or feedback issue resolved should log into sop_training_updates:
 Workflow:
 Ops Lead reviews flagged feedback weekly.
 If valid --> create/update SOP (auto-tracked in table).
 Assign action_owner.
 If training required --> staff automatically enrolled in module (LMS integration).
⚡ Developer Note: Use a Jira/Trello integration so updates create real training tasks.

6. KPI Dashboard Integration
The KPIs pull live data from all the above:
 From Feedback: Average ratings, recurring issues, response times.
 From Risk Register: # of open risks, avg. time to mitigation.
 From Playbooks: Drill frequency, response time.
 From SOP Updates: # of updates pushed, % implemented.
Visualize in PowerBI, Metabase, or Superset with RAG (Red/Amber/Green) indicators.
Example Metrics:
 Speed-to-Response (signal --> solution).
 % of flagged feedback closed within SLA.
 of new SOP updates per quarter.
 Tenant Reliability Index (linked to TRI module).

7. Quarterly Rhythm Integration
Build this cadence into system:
 Q1: Risk Register review --> auto-generate report.
 Q2: Fire Drill (system enforces playbook simulation).
 Q3: Monthly Learning Reviews (pull top-3 feedback issues auto-flagged).
 Q4: Financial Cushion Check (system matches balance sheet vs. target).
 
Technical Design: Multi-Professional & Professional Team Management
Objective: To allow the assignment of multiple verification specialists (Professionals, lawyers, engineers) to a single transaction, each with their own specific tasks, deadlines, and permissions.
1. Enhanced Data Models
The core change is to move from a one-to-one relationship between a Transaction and a Professional to a one-to-many relationship via a new tasks table.
New/Modified Database Tables
a. professionals Table (Replaces/extends Professionals)
This table stores all types of verifiers and professionals.
Field
Type
Description
id
UUID
Primary Key
user_id
UUID (FK)
Links to base user account (for login)
type
Enum
Professional, lawyer, surveyor, engineer, contractor
specialization
String
e.g., "Title Verification", "Construction", "Commercial Property"
rating
Decimal
Average rating from completed tasks
license_number
String
For lawyers, surveyors, etc.
b. tasks Table (The Core of the New System)
This table manages every discrete unit of work in a transaction.
Field
Type
Description
id
UUID

transaction_id
UUID (FK)
The parent transaction
professional_id
UUID (FK)
Who is assigned the task
title
String
e.g., "Verify Title Deed at Registry", "Conduct Structural Inspection"
description
Text
Detailed instructions
status
Enum
pending, assigned, in_progress, completed, failed
due_date
DateTime

required_proof
JSON
e.g., ["photo", "video", "pdf_report"] - what they must upload
is_milestone
Boolean
If true, completing this task is required to release funds.
c. task_evidence Table
Stores the proof uploaded by professionals for each task.
Field
Type
Description
id
UUID

task_id
UUID (FK)

file_url
String
Link to the file (image, PDF, video) in storage (S3)
notes
Text
Comments from the professional
verified_by_system
Boolean
Could be used later for AI checks
2. How It Works: Dynamic Task Assignment
The system can now automatically assign multiple tasks to different professionals based on the transaction's needs.
Scenario: A diaspora buyer is purchasing a piece of land to build a house.
 Transaction is created.
 System automatically creates tasks based on rules:
 IF transaction_type == "land_purchase" THEN
 CREATE_TASK: title="Title Deed Verification", professional_type="lawyer", is_milestone=true
 CREATE_TASK: title="Site Survey & Boundary Identification", professional_type="surveyor", is_milestone=true
 CREATE_TASK: title="Soil Test", professional_type="engineer", is_milestone=false (optional but recommended)
Diagram

3. The Participant Experience
For the Buyer/Seller/Agent: Their dashboard becomes even more transparent. They see a detailed checklist of all tasks required for their specific transaction, who is responsible for each, and the status.
View in Buyer's Dashboard:
text
Transaction: Land Purchase in Karen
Overall Status: Verification In Progress (2 of 3 tasks complete)

Task Checklist:
✅ [Lawyer] Title Deed Verification - Completed May 25
   - [View Legal Report PDF]
✅ [Surveyor] Boundary Survey - Completed May 28
   - [View Surveyor's Map] [View Site Photos]
🟡 [Engineer] Soil Test - In Progress (Due June 2)
   - Assigned to: Eng. David M.
[Release Funds] Button is DISABLED until milestone tasks are complete.
For the Professional (Lawyer, Professional, etc.): They log into their dashboard and only see the tasks assigned to them. Their interface is simple and focused.
Professional's Task View:
text
Your Tasks:
1. Task: Soil Test for [Land in Karen]
   - Transaction: SL-TXN-67890
   - Due: June 2
   - Instructions: Drill 3 boreholes and test soil composition.
   - Required Proof: Upload PDF report and photos of each borehole.

Actions: [Start Task] [Upload Evidence] [Request More Info]
4. Inviting External Professionals
The system needs a robust invitation workflow.
 Automatic Assignment: For professionals already in your system (e.g., a vetted lawyer on your panel), the system assigns the task directly to them.
 Manual Invitation: For a unique specialist required by the client (e.g., "I want my own lawyer to review"), the Agent or Buyer can:
 Click "Invite a Professional".
 Enter the professional's email and name.
 Select their type (e.g., "lawyer").
 The system sends an email invitation with a link to register on the platform and access the specific task.
5. Developer Implementation Notes
 State Machine: The transactions.escrow_status must now be gated on the tasks table. A transaction can only move to ready_for_release if all tasks where is_milestone = true have a status of completed.
 Notifications: The system must emit events (TaskCreated, TaskCompleted, TaskOverdue) to trigger notifications to the relevant parties (agent, buyer, professional).
 Payment Logic: The professionals table needs a payment_rate field. Upon task completion, their fee is calculated and reserved to be paid from the transaction fees upon successful completion.
 API Endpoint: Need a new API endpoint for professionals to fetch their tasks and upload evidence.
text
GET /api/professionals/tasks
POST /api/tasks/{task_id}/evidence


Hybrid Registration & Onboarding Framework
(Secure Lead-to-Client Funnel with Management Model Integration + Risk, Probation & Audit Enhancements)
Strategic Objective:
To balance open lead capture with secure, audited onboarding. Secure Living's operational dashboard remains completely inaccessible until a user is fully verified and oriented.
Business Model Principle:
 Secure Living is the default property manager (manager_type = secure_living).
 Landlords may opt into self-management, with the option to appoint delegates.
 Delegation, payouts, and sensitive actions are strictly limited by scope, OTP, expiry rules, and audit trails.

1. Registration Tiers & Access Walls
Tier 1  -  Self-Service Application Portal (Lead Funnel)
Purpose: Public-facing intake for Tenants, Landlords, Diaspora Clients, and Professionals.
Access: ZERO dashboard access.
Process:
 User lands on branded landing page (e.g., secureliving.co.ke/apply-now).
 Fills out application form (tenant, landlord, diaspora, or pro).
 After submission --> thank-you message:
"Thank you, our team will contact you for verification."
 Lead is logged in CRM / Sales pipeline.
 No credentials generated at this stage.

Tier 2  -  Admin-Assisted Onboarding (Client Conversion)
Purpose: Only trusted, verified users enter the platform.
Access: Full dashboard unlocked only after:
✔️ Document Verification --> ✔️ Orientation --> ✔️ Approval.
Process:
 Admin reviews Tier 1 lead.
 Admin promotes applicant --> status = pending_client.
 Secure Invitation sent (link valid 72 hours).
 User uploads mandatory verification documents.
 Staff reviews & approves --> system triggers orientation.
 On completion --> system issues credentials.

2. Self-Service Application Portal (User-Facing)
Tenants (Prospective)
 Name, Phone, Email
 Desired Location & Rent Budget
 Employment Information
 Upload ID/Passport (optional here, mandatory later)
 Consent Checkbox:
"I agree to be contacted for verification."
Landlords (Prospective)
 Name, Contact Info
 Property Portfolio Summary (# properties, locations)
 Service Preference:
 [✔] Secure Living Management (default)
 Self-Manage (DIY)
 If Self-Manage --> option to appoint delegate later.
External Professionals (Prospective)
 Name / Company Name, Contact Info
 Service Category (dropdown: Surveyor, Lawyer, Plumber, etc.)
 License Number / Years of Experience
 Checkbox:
"Apply to Join Secure Living Professional Network."
Diaspora Clients
 Personal + Property Details
 Country of Residence & Embassy/Consulate (for ID notarization)
 Preferred Communication Channel (Video KYC, Email, or Phone)
Post-Submission Rules:
 No login generated.
 Auto-email acknowledgment: "We've received your application."
 Application frozen until Admin review.

3. Secure Client Activation Pathway (Admin-Assisted)
Step 1  -  Secure Invitation
 Admin sends secure link (valid 72 hrs).
 Required Uploads:
 Tenants/Landlords: ID + Lease/Title doc.
 Diaspora Clients: Embassy-certified ID + Proof of Address Abroad.
 Professionals: License, Portfolio, Insurance Certificate.
Step 2  -  Verification Layer
 Staff reviews uploads.
 Fraud checks:
 Live photo + ID match.
 Bank validation (micro-deposit confirmation for payout accounts).
 System auto-generates a risk_score (fraud/AML hooks).
Step 3  -  Mandatory Orientation (Training Modules)
 Tenants: "How to Pay Rent via Escrow" + quiz.
 Landlords: "Dashboard & Payout Rules" + dispute-handling simulation.
 Diaspora Clients: "Remote Oversight & Delegation" + mandatory video call with Secure Living staff.
 Professionals: "Escrow & Communication Standards" module.
Task Gating: Users cannot raise disputes, request payouts, or initiate service tasks until orientation is passed.
Step 4  -  Full Access Grant
 On passing orientation:
 Email sent:
"Your account has been verified. Click here to set your password."
 Role-based dashboard access unlocked.
 Immutable audit logging begins.

4. Management Model  --  Secure Living Default vs. Self-Manage
Default Mode (Secure Living Managed)
 manager_type = secure_living
 Secure Living handles:
 Rent collection.
 Payouts.
 Maintenance.
 Inspections.
Optional: Self-Manage Mode
 manager_type = self_manage
 Landlord options:
 DIY (manage themselves).
 Appoint Delegate (Local Manager or Third Party).
Delegation Rules
 Delegates require:
✔️ Verification (KYC).
✔️ Explicit Landlord Approval.
 Permissions scope-limited:
 view_only (read-only).
 payout_limited (KES 50,000 cap default).
 full (but >KES 100k requires Step-Up OTP/video KYC).
 Auto-expiry: default 1 year.
 Audit Trails: every delegate action is logged.
 Auto-Revoke: If landlord account is suspended, all delegations terminate instantly.
Escrow Logic
 All tenant payments --> Secure Living escrow wallet.
 Payout rules:
 <= cap --> delegate approval valid.
 cap --> Step-Up landlord approval required.
 If landlord unresponsive --> auto-escalation to Secure Living Supervisor.

5. Security Overlays & Audit Controls
 Immutable Audit Logs
Every action (signup, ID upload, payout approval, dispute) logged permanently.
 Step-Up Authentication
Required for:
 Assigning/revoking managers.
 High-value withdrawals (>KES 100,000).
 Contract sign-offs.
 Probation for Clients
 New Tenants/Landlords flagged as probationary_client.
 Initial payout cap (KES 50,000).
 Auto-review after 3 months activity.
 Fraud/AML Hooks
 risk_score auto-updated by patterns (e.g., same ID across multiple accounts, repeated chargebacks).
 Risk-based escalation to compliance team.
 Suspension Rules
 Fake ID, payment fraud, or ToS breach --> immediate suspension.
 Funds/tasks auto-frozen until resolution.
 Exit Report
 Generated automatically on termination.
 Includes tasks, funds, pending approvals, and audit trail.

6. Example Workflows
A. Tenant Lead --> Verified Client
 Tenant applies via portal.
 Admin promotes --> uploads ID + Lease.
 Completes escrow training quiz.
 Login issued --> can pay rent, request maintenance.
B. Landlord (Secure Living Managed)
 Applies --> selects "Managed by Secure Living."
 Staff verifies ownership docs.
 Completes payout policy module.
 Dashboard unlocked (revenue reports, payouts).
C. Landlord (Self-Manage with Delegate)
 Opts for self-manage.
 Invites delegate --> delegate completes KYC.
 Landlord approves via OTP.
 Delegate granted payout cap (KES 50,000).
 Delegate approves minor tasks; high payouts escalate.
D. Diaspora Client
 Uploads embassy-certified ID.
 Bank micro-deposit validation.
 Completes orientation via video call.
 Dashboard unlocked (remote monitoring, delegation).
E. Professional (External)
 Applies via portal.
 Admin verifies license.
 Completes escrow communication module.
 Dashboard unlocked (assignments enabled).

7. Technical Implementation
Database Schema (Expanded)
clients (
  client_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  type ENUM('tenant','landlord','external_pro','diaspora_client'),
  status ENUM('lead','pending','verified','probation','suspended'),
  manager_type ENUM('secure_living','self_manage'),
  verification_docs JSONB,
  orientation_passed BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
  probation_cap NUMERIC DEFAULT 50000, -- probation payout limit
  risk_score NUMERIC DEFAULT 0,        -- fraud/AML scoring
  created_at TIMESTAMP
);

delegations (
  delegation_id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  landlord_id UUID,
  delegate_id UUID,
  scope ENUM('view_only','payout_limited','full'),
  approval_limit NUMERIC,
  expires_at DATE,
  created_by UUID, -- Admin
  approval_evidence JSONB -- store OTP logs, KYC session ID, etc.
);

audit_logs (
  log_id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id UUID,
  role VARCHAR,
  action_type VARCHAR,
  details JSONB,
  ip_address INET,
  created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT now()
);

✅ Final Outcome:
This framework gives you:
 A spam-proof lead funnel for marketing.
 A vault-like onboarding gate for verified clients.
 Secure Living default management model, with flexible delegation for advanced clients.
 Enterprise-grade fraud prevention (probation, AML scoring, audit trails).
 A developer-ready schema for implementation.





🔐 Unified Registration, Onboarding, Probation & Termination Framework
(For Internal Staff, Contractors, Professionals, Tenants, Landlords, Diaspora Clients)
Strategic Objective:
To design a single lifecycle engine that governs every user type in the Secure Living ecosystem  --  internal (staff, supervisors, contractors) and external (tenants, landlords, diaspora clients, professionals).
 Core Principle: Everyone passes through the same four stages (Application --> Verification --> Orientation --> Active Lifecycle), with tailored rules.
 Super Admin Oversight: The super admin has omnipotent powers to play any role but all overrides require Step-Up authentication and are immutably logged.
 Result: One scalable framework, maximum security, minimum duplication.

1. Registration & Account Creation
A. Internal Users (HR/Staff/Professionals/Contractors)
Initiator: HR/Admin creates account.
Security:
 Step-Up Authentication required.
 Dual approval for sensitive roles (Finance, Supervisors, Escrow Managers).
 Device & Location Binding (probationary staff can only log in from whitelisted devices/locations).
Probation Default: 90 days, auto-suspends if not confirmed.
Contractors: Start/end date required. Auto-disable at expiry.

B. External Clients (Tenants, Landlords, Diaspora, Professionals)
Two-Tier Entry System (Marketing Funnel --> Secure Client Conversion):
 Tier 1: Self-Service Application Portal
 Public-facing page (e.g., secureliving.co.ke/apply-now).
 Users fill application (Tenants --> location & budget, Landlords --> portfolio, Professionals --> license & experience, Diaspora --> embassy-certified ID & property details).
 ZERO dashboard access at this stage.
 Stored in CRM.
 Tier 2: Admin-Assisted Onboarding
 Admin reviews & promotes applicant to "Pending Client."
 Secure Invitation (72hr validity) sent via email/SMS.
 User uploads verification docs (ID, lease/title, embassy certification, license).
 Verification checks: live photo match, bank micro-deposit validation, fraud checks.
 On approval --> mandatory orientation training --> dashboard unlocked.

2. Onboarding & Training (Quality Enforcement Layer)
 Mandatory Orientation (Role-Specific Modules):
 Tenants: "How to Pay Rent via Escrow" video + quiz.
 Landlords: "Revenue Payouts & Dispute Handling" module.
 Diaspora Clients: "Remote Oversight & Delegate Management" + video call Q&A.
 Professionals: "Escrow & Client Communication Protocols."
 Staff: "Inspection Standards" & "Data Quality Rules."
 Supervisors: "Approval Workflows & Audit Compliance."
 Task/Feature Gating:
 Until training completed, sensitive features (withdrawals, property creation, escrow handling, inspections) remain 🔒.
 Example: Tenant cannot request refunds until they pass escrow training.
 Gamification:
 🎓 Badges on completion.
 Leaderboards (internal staff).
 Orientation KPI tracked for compliance.

3. Probation & Performance Tracking
 Applies to: Internal staff, contractors, and new clients (tenants/landlords) during first 90 days.
 Visual Flag: 🔶 probation tag on dashboards.
 KPIs Monitored:
 Staff: SLA compliance, rework rate, data quality, satisfaction scores.
 Tenants: Payment timeliness, fraud checks.
 Landlords: Responsiveness, dispute escalations.
 Professionals: Completion rate, ratings, documentation quality.
 AI Recommendation Engine:
 "✅ SLA 97%, Satisfaction 94% --> Recommend Confirmation."
 "⚠️ Declining performance, 3 flagged anomalies --> Recommend Termination/Review."
 Super Admin Power: Can override probation outcomes, but all logged with super_admin_override = TRUE.

4. Active Work Lifecycle
Internal Users
 Contractors:
 Bound to contract dates.
 Grace period configurable (e.g., 24h).
 Mid-task expiry --> auto-reassignment or escalation.
 Staff & Supervisors:
 Continuous task assignment.
 KPI tracking with dashboards.
 Retraining auto-assigned when policies/system update.
External Clients
 Tenants:
 Pay rent into escrow.
 Submit requests (repairs, vacating, disputes).
 Landlords:
 Default: Secure Living manages properties.
 Optional: Self-manage --> assign delegate (scoped: view_only, payout_limited, full).
 Diaspora Clients:
 Enhanced remote verification (embassy docs, bank validation).
 High-value withdrawals (>KES 100,000) require Step-Up approval (OTP/Video KYC).
 Delegates capped at payout authority <= KES 50,000.
 Professionals:
 Receive assignments.
 Submit quotes, milestones, and reports.
 Ratings & quality thresholds tracked.

5. Termination & Offboarding
Trigger: HR/Admin initiates workflow with Step-Up Auth.
Immediate Actions:
 Access suspension.
 Sessions destroyed.
 Escrow/finance rights revoked.
Intelligent Task Handling:
 Non-critical --> reassigned to pool.
 Critical --> auto-assign to skill-matched staff/professional. If none, escalate to Supervisor.
Data Handling:
 Cached data wiped.
 Immutable audit logs stored.
 Historical work retained.
Exit Report: Sent to HR/Admin with:
 Tasks reassigned.
 Pending approvals.
 Training/performance archive.
External Users (Tenants/Landlords/Pros):
 Suspension triggers: fake docs, fraud, chargebacks.
 Escrow funds frozen pending investigation.
 Properties or tasks paused until resolution.

6. Security Overlays & Accountability
 Immutable Audit Logs:
Every action (hire, verification, payouts, termination) logged with timestamp, IP, and justification.
 Step-Up Authentication:
Required for:
 Financial approvals.
 Role creation/termination.
 High-value withdrawals.
 Contract sign-offs.
 Delegation Controls:
 Delegates require explicit approval & expiry.
 Capped authority (default payout <= KES 50,000).
 All actions logged.
 Regulatory Reporting:
Auto-generate AML/CFT compliance packs.
 Super Admin Oversight:
 Can assume any role (HR, Landlord, Supervisor, Finance).
 Every override requires second password/biometric.
 Overrides flagged in logs for compliance.

7. Example Unified Workflows
A. Tenant Lead --> Verified Client
 Tenant applies via portal.
 Admin promotes --> uploads ID + lease.
 Tenant passes escrow quiz.
 Dashboard unlocked --> rent payments enabled.
B. Staff Hire --> Probation --> Confirmation
 HR creates account with probation.
 Staff completes "Inspection Standards."
 SLA 96%, Satisfaction 92%.
 HR confirms, probation tag removed.
C. Contractor Expiry Mid-Task
 Contract ends 31 Mar.
 Contractor has 1 pending repair ticket.
 Task auto-reassigned to backup staff.
 Account disabled Apr 1, midnight.
D. Diaspora Client Withdrawal
 Diaspora client requests KES 200,000 withdrawal.
 System requests Step-Up OTP + Video KYC.
 On approval, funds released.
 Logged with audit tag: high_value_withdrawal.
E. Termination with Critical Task
 Supervisor initiates termination of Staff_X.
 System finds 2 active tasks.
 One minor --> reassigned quietly.
 One critical --> escalated to Electrical Specialist.
 Exit report sent to HR + Admin.

8. Technical Implementation
users (
  user_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  type ENUM('staff','supervisor','contractor','tenant','landlord','diaspora','professional'),
  status ENUM('lead','pending','probation','active','suspended','terminated'),
  manager_type ENUM('secure_living','self_manage'),
  probation_end_date DATE,
  contract_start_date DATE,
  contract_end_date DATE,
  approval_limit NUMERIC,
  created_by UUID,
  created_at TIMESTAMP
);

delegations (
  delegation_id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  landlord_id UUID,
  delegate_id UUID,
  scope ENUM('view_only','payout_limited','full'),
  approval_limit NUMERIC,
  expires_at DATE,
  created_by UUID
);

audit_logs (
  log_id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  action_type VARCHAR,
  performed_by UUID,
  target_user UUID,
  justification TEXT,
  ip_address VARCHAR,
  super_admin_override BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
  timestamp TIMESTAMP
);
UI/UX Hooks
 Probation Badge 🔶 in all dashboards.
 Locked Tasks 🔒 until training passed.
 Delegation Tree visualization with expiry timers.
 Super Admin Override ⚡ marker in all audit logs.
 Termination Pop-Up with mandatory justification.

✅ Final Outcome
 One unified lifecycle engine for ALL users.
 Flexible enough for staff, contractors, landlords, diaspora clients, and professionals.
 Security-first: Step-Up Auth, audit logs, delegation controls.
 Accountability guaranteed: immutable logs, exit reports, probation tracking.
 Scalable: new user roles can be added by simply updating the taxonomy + onboarding modules.











🔐 Secure Living Unified User Lifecycle Framework
(Internal HR + Client Onboarding + Security Overlays)

🎯 Strategic Objective
To establish a comprehensive, secure, and auditable framework for managing every type of user interacting with Secure Living.
This framework unifies:
 Internal HR Lifecycle (Staff, Contractors, Supervisors, Admins)
 Client/External User Lifecycle (Tenants, Landlords, Diaspora, Professionals)
 Security Overlays (Risk Scoring, Permission Matrix, Immutable Audit Logs, Super Admin Safeguards)
The outcome is a flexible but tightly controlled platform where all actions are tracked, permissions are contextual, and accountability is enforced end-to-end.
All HR lifecycle actions (hiring, onboarding, probation, termination) remain subject to Super Admin oversight as defined

1. 🔹 Unified User Type Taxonomy
A single taxonomy ensures no ambiguity in role classification.
user_type ENUM(
  -- Internal (HR Lifecycle)
  'staff_ops', 'staff_supervisor', 'staff_finance', 'staff_admin',
  'contractor_verified', 'contractor_temporary',

  -- Clients (External)
  'tenant_residential', 'tenant_commercial', 
  'landlord_individual', 'landlord_corporate',
  'diaspora_verified', 'diaspora_premium',
  
  -- Professionals
  'professional_legal', 'professional_technical',

  -- System Roles
  'super_admin'
)
🔎 Why?
 Removes overlap between role (job function) and user type (classification).
 Helps create granular rules for permissions, onboarding, risk scoring, and reporting.

2. 🔹 Registration & Onboarding Flows
Tier 1: Public Application Portal (Lead Funnel)
 Open to public; no dashboard access.
 Purpose: Lead capture for Tenants, Landlords, Professionals, Diaspora clients.
 Hosted separately from the main app.
Process
 User fills a public form (name, contact, category, service preferences).
 Stored in CRM as status = lead.
 Auto-email confirmation: "Thank you for applying. A Secure Living staff will contact you."
👉 This ensures zero spam or bots entering the core system.

Tier 2: Admin-Assisted Promotion (Pending Client)
 Admin reviews lead and promotes to Pending Client.
 Secure invitation link (valid 72h) sent via email/SMS.
 User uploads mandatory KYC docs (different per type).
Document Requirements
 Tenants: ID + Lease Document.
 Landlords: ID + Title Deed Extract / Ownership Proof.
 Diaspora: Embassy-certified ID + Proof of Address Abroad.
 Professionals: License + Insurance + References.
👉 At this point, status = pending_verification.

Tier 3: Verification Layer
 Staff reviews documents.
 Fraud checks (optional: ID-photo live match, bank validation via micro-deposit).
 If rejected --> return to lead stage with explanation.
 If approved --> move to pending_orientation.

Tier 4: Mandatory Orientation
 Orientation modules role-specific:
User Type
Training Module
Unlock Condition
Tenant_Residential
How to Pay Rent via Escrow video + quiz
Unlock rent payment
Tenant_Commercial
Bulk Rent Uploads & Escrow Rules
Unlock batch processing

Unlock payout dashboard
Landlord_Corporate
Delegation & Audit Controls training
Unlock delegation panel

Remote Oversight Guide + Video KYC
Unlock monitoring
